! Title: AdBlock Filter Lite
! Description: 适用于 AdGuard 的去广告合并规则，每 8 个小时更新一次。规则源：AdGuard Chinese filter、AdRules DNS List、anti-AD、Hagezi Pro、Hagezi Threat Intelligence、秋风广告过滤规则、CJX's Annoyance List、xinggsf rule、xinggsf mv、ADgk、大圣净化 Purple、jiekouAD、OISD Basic。Lite 版仅针对国内域名拦截。
